## Intent

**Problem:** `README.md` is empty, so the interviewer cannot run or judge the submission (CAP-5). The suite passes but `npm test` reports an unhandled `Invalid LatLng object: (NaN, NaN)` error, the keyboard list → details → back flow is only covered in two halves, and the park-to-park test deferred from 1.3 is still missing.

**Approach:** Close the test gaps against the Story 1.6 ACs with test-only changes, so `npm test` is clean, and write the submission README with every section the AC lists. Story 1.5 is being built in parallel on its own branch; this story does not touch app code, and the user rebases onto 1.5 later.

## Boundaries & Constraints

**Always:** test-only changes under `src/`; every README claim is true of the code at this commit or labelled as pending Story 1.5; README run instructions are exactly `npm install` then `npm run dev` (plus `npm test`, `npm run build`); name `parks.sample.json` as unchanged; state "no API keys, no geolocation".

**Never:** edit `src/**/*.jsx`/`src/data/*.js` app code, `index.css`, or `parks.sample.json`; layout/responsive/a11y polish (1.5); new dependencies; invent features in "What works"; edit or redact transcript contents.

**Decisions (human):**
- Time spent: "about 2 hours" — started prompting ~9 pm, agents ran while the developer stepped away, ~15 min of hands-on work before the first commit just before 10 pm, still working at 11:25 pm.
- Story 1.5: the README describes this branch truthfully; 1.5 items (desktop sidebar, phone List/Map toggle, responsive/a11y polish, design applied) are marked "in progress (Story 1.5)" for the developer to update at rebase.
- Transcripts move with `git mv` into `transcripts/`, keeping extensions: `transcript1.txt` → `01-project-scaffolding.txt`, `2` → `02-story1-dev`, `3` → `03-story2-dev`, `4` → `04-story2-review`, `5` → `05-story3-dev`, `6` → `06-story3-review`, `7` → `07-story4-dev-review`; `claude-design-transcript.md` → `design-transcript.md`; `claude-session-extraction-sidequest-transcript.md` → `tool-transcript.md`. `merge_session_transcripts.py` → `tools/`. The README states the `NN-storyN-<dev|review>` convention and that transcripts 08+ follow it.

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |
|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|
| Clean run | `npm test` | all tests pass, 0 unhandled errors | N/A |
| Keyboard flow | `/`, Tab until Old Mill's card has focus, Enter | details `<h1>` focused; Shift+Tab, Enter on "All parks" → focus on Old Mill's card | N/A |
| Park → park | desktop, open Prospect Park, fire `error` on its image, click marker for another park with images | new park's `<img>` renders and its `<h1>` has focus | N/A |
| Repeat marker click | the existing duplicate-history test | passes with no Leaflet NaN error | test-only |

</frozen-after-approval>

## Code Map

- `src/App.test.jsx` -- `renderAt`, `renderWithLocation`, `stubData`, `stubViewport`, `markerNamed`/`findMarkerByName`, `cardFor`, `findCards`. Line ~533 "All parks" test opens by click; ~261 Enter test does not check heading focus or return; ~713 duplicate-history test is the NaN source.
- NaN root cause: two clicks on a marker within 200 ms make Leaflet (`leaflet-src.js` `simDblclick`) synthesize a `dblclick` on the map; `mouseEventToContainerPoint` divides by jsdom's zero `getBoundingClientRect` width → NaN. A jsdom artefact, not an app bug.
- `src/data/parks.test.js:65` -- frozen-source no-mutation test of the image transform already exists; keep it.
- `_bmad-output/specs/spec-find-local-parks/SPEC.md` (Assumptions), `park-data.md` (gaps, image transform), `stack.md`, `epic-1-context.md` (decisions) -- README sources. Spec Implementation Notes of 1.1–1.4 hold dev decisions (fetch from `public/data/`, React Router, vitest 4/jsdom 29 on Node 25, divIcon markers, `tile.openstreetmap.org`).
- `deferred-work.md` -- the 1.3 park→park entry; mark it resolved.

  - **Test noise documented instead of fixed (line 90):** The jsdom `scrollTo` "Not implemented" notices are listed as a known issue. Stubbing `window.scrollTo` once in the Vitest setup file would remove them. It would also stop the noise hiding real console errors in CI output.
  - **Assumptions section is incomplete (lines 72–78):** Several assumptions the app relies on are listed only under Decisions or Left out, or not at all. These are: the 768px desktop/phone breakpoint, A–Z ordering as the numbering source, showing only the first photo, and treating a missing `matchMedia` as desktop. The last one is visible in the tests, not the README. A reader checking assumptions would miss them.
</agent-message>

08-story5-dev-review.txt
---
title: 'Story 1.5: Responsive and accessibility polish with design applied'
type: 'feature'
created: '2026-10-14'
baseline_commit: 'ea328a0651f3c7c34484d65f6ed775576d9786e9'
status: 'done'
route: 'dispatch'

context:
  - '{project-root}/_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/epic-1-context.md'
---

<frozen-after-approval reason="human-owned intent — do not modify unless human renegotiates">

## Intent

**Problem:** The shell stacks header, list and a fixed 480px map in one column at every width. There is no desktop sidebar/map split, no phone List/Map toggle, no phone details header, and sizes and spacing drift from `design/README.md` (CAP-4 full pass).

**Approach:** Restyle the shell to the designed layouts. On desktop (≥ 768px), show a green header, a 400px independently scrolling sidebar and an edge-to-edge map filling the rest. On phone, show one column with a List / Map toggle (List default), and details full screen with "‹ All parks" in the green header. Then align sizes with the design and close the accessibility checklist, including Leaflet's own controls.

## Boundaries & Constraints

**Always:** keep the landmarks `<header>`, `<main>`, `<aside aria-label="Parks">` and `<section aria-label="Map of parks">`; exactly one `<h1>` per view, including loading/error; every interactive element (Leaflet zoom buttons included) shows the amber focus ring and is ≥ 44×44px; the toggle is two `<button type="button">`s with `aria-pressed`; keep 1.2–1.4 behaviour (heading focus, card focus return, marker activation by `useIsDesktop`, preview, marker refocus); no horizontal scroll at 375px; OSM attribution stays visible.

**Never:** new map features (panning on select, clustering); search, filters or geolocation; editing `parks.sample.json` or `src/data/parks.js`; a component library. Layout switches with Tailwind `md:` classes. `useIsDesktop` keeps deciding behaviour only.

**Decisions (agent-made, user-invisible or design-permitted):**
- The header has an `aria-hidden` tree icon and the app name as a `<p>`. On desktop it reads "Parks in the New York City area" with "12 parks" on the right; on phone, "12 parks · New York City area".
- On phone details the header shows only the "‹ All parks" link, and the sidebar's bordered back link is desktop-only.
- Loading/error sit under an "All parks" `<h1>`.
- `document.title` is "Find a Park" on the list and "{name} · Find a Park" on details.
- The toggle state lives in `App` and is ignored at desktop width.
- Switching to List closes any open preview.

**Decisions (human):** On phone, Back from details opened via the map (marker → preview → "View park details") returns to the Map tab with focus on that park's marker. Back from details opened via the list returns to the List tab with focus on the card. The tab the details were opened from is remembered in `App` state, so browser Back works too.

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |
|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|
| Phone list default | `/`, < 768px | List `aria-pressed="true"`, Map `"false"`; map pane hidden | N/A |
| Phone Map tab | press Map | Map pressed; list pane hidden; map pane shown and resized to fill it (tiles not grey) | N/A |
| Phone details | `/parks/:id`, < 768px | no toggle, no map; header "All parks" link; one `<h1>` | N/A |
| Desktop | ≥ 768px, any route | header with count; 400px aside scrolls alone; map fills rest; no toggle | N/A |
| Resize | phone Map tab → desktop → phone | both panes on desktop; map re-sizes; no stale preview | N/A |
| Loading / error | fetch pending / rejects | "All parks" `<h1>` + status card | Retry works |

</frozen-after-approval>

## Code Map

- `src/App.jsx` -- shell to restructure: header, `main`, `aside className="p-5"`, map `section` with a `h-[480px]` wrapper around `ParkMap` + `ParkPreview`. Reuse `selectedId` (`useMatch`), `isDesktop`, `previewId`, `focusMarkerId`, `lastParkId`. `ParksPanel`, `NotFound`, `ParkDetailsRoute` stay.
- `src/components/ParkMap.jsx` -- `MapContainer` has `rounded-[10px]`; drop the rounding for edge-to-edge. Add a child that calls `map.invalidateSize()` from a `ResizeObserver` on the container (Leaflet measures 0×0 while `display:none`).
- `src/components/ParkPreview.jsx` -- unchanged unless sizes drift from screen 05.
- `src/components/ParkList.jsx` -- `h1` 22px/800; card gap 10px; card padding 14px; badge 30px; tags 13px `px-2 py-[3px]`; address 15px. Keep one `<Link>` per card and the `focusParkId` effect.
- `src/components/ParkDetails.jsx` -- back link `hidden md:inline-flex`; set `document.title` in the `park.id` effect; tile `dt` 13px.
- `src/index.css` -- add `--color-toggle-bg: #E6E2D8`; body 15px. Restyle `.leaflet-control-zoom a` to 44×44 with the amber `:focus-visible` outline.
- `design/source/PhoneList.dc.html`, `PhoneDetails.dc.html`, `Main.dc.html` -- exact values:
  - Header: padding 14px 24px (phone 14px 16px); name 20px/800 (phone 18px); subtitle 14px (phone 13px `#D7E8DD`).
  - Toggle: track `#E6E2D8`, 4px padding/gap, 10px radius, margin 12px 16px. Selected button white, 700, shadow; unselected transparent, 600, `#3F4A44`.
  - Phone details header: 8px padding; link 16px/600.
- `design/screens/01`, `02`, `04`, `05`, `06` -- visual targets.
- `src/App.test.jsx` -- `renderAt`, `okResponse`, `TestNav`, `matchMedia` stubs from 1.4. Tests use `getByRole('heading', { level: 1 })` and click "All parks".

## Tasks & Acceptance

**Execution:**
- [x] `src/index.css` -- toggle token, body size, Leaflet zoom-control size and focus ring.
- [x] `src/components/ParkMap.jsx` -- edge-to-edge, and a resize-to-container hook.
- [x] `src/App.jsx` -- add a `Header` (normal vs phone-details), a `ViewToggle` (`md:hidden`, absent on details), and an `h-dvh` flex layout. The aside is `md:w-[400px] md:shrink-0 md:overflow-y-auto p-4 md:p-5`. The map section is `flex-1 relative`, hidden on phone when List is active or details are open. Add the loading/error `<h1>`, the list `document.title`, and the Back-from-details behaviour per the human decision (return to Map tab + marker focus, or List tab + card focus).
- [x] `src/components/ParkList.jsx`, `src/components/ParkDetails.jsx` -- sizes/spacing above; the desktop-only sidebar back link; details title.
- [x] `src/App.test.jsx`, `src/components/ParkMap.test.jsx` -- toggle `aria-pressed` and pane `hidden` classes; one `<h1>` in list, details, loading, error and not-found; the header back link returns focus; `document.title`; switching to List closes the preview; phone Back from map-opened details restores the Map tab and focuses the marker; `invalidateSize` runs on resize.

**Acceptance Criteria:**
- Given any view, then exactly one `<h1>` exists and no `div`/`span` has a click handler.
- Given 375px and 1280px in a real browser, when the user does list → details → back by keyboard, then there is no horizontal scroll and each focused element shows the amber ring.
- Given the existing suites, then they still pass, with selectors changed only where duplicate link names require it.

## Design Notes

Two "All parks" links exist on details (phone header, desktop sidebar). Each is CSS-hidden at the other width, so a real browser exposes one. jsdom applies no CSS, so tests pick one with `within(header)` or `within(aside)`.

# Find a Park

A small web app that helps residents find a local park in the NYC-area sample data, on a map or in a list, and open its details. Built with React + Vite as a 2-hour take-home.

No API keys, no geolocation: the app never asks for the resident's location, and every service it uses is keyless.

## Run

Requires Node.js 22.22 or newer (React Router 8 requires it) and npm; developed on Node 25.

```sh
npm install
npm run dev
```

Then open the URL Vite prints (usually http://localhost:5173).

Other commands:

```sh
npm test        # Vitest + React Testing Library, single run
npm run build   # production build into dist/
```

## What works

On this branch:

- **Data load with loading, error and retry states.** "Loading parks…" while the JSON loads; "Couldn't load parks. Try again." with a Retry button if it fails, never a blank screen.
- **List of all 12 parks**, A–Z and numbered 1–12, including the ones with missing fields. Each card is one link (one Tab stop) with a number badge, name, address (or "Address not listed") and the first three amenities plus "+N more".
- **Keyboard selection.** Tab to a card, Enter opens its details, focus moves to the details heading; "All parks" (or browser Back) returns to the list with focus on the card you came from.
- **Park details** at `/parks/:id`: "All parks" back link, photo ("Photo 1 of N"), name, address, "Open in OpenStreetMap" link, Hours / Size / Rating tiles, About, and all amenities. Unknown ids show "Park not found" with a link back.
- **Missing data handled honestly**, never invented: "Address not listed", "No description has been provided for this park.", "Not yet rated" (never 0 stars), "Not listed" for hours and acreage, "No amenities listed", a dashed "No photos for this park yet" box for empty or failing images, and no marker or OSM link for a park without coordinates.
- **Map of parks** (Leaflet + OpenStreetMap tiles, attribution visible). The initial view fits all parks with coordinates. Each marker is a focusable button named "N. Park name", sharing the list's numbering; the selected park's marker is amber and larger.
  - Desktop (≥ 768px): clicking or pressing Enter on a marker opens that park's details.
  - Phone (< 768px): tapping a marker opens a preview card ("Park N of 12", name, address, hours, "View park details", "Close park preview"); Escape or the close button returns focus to the marker.
- **Visible amber focus ring** on the app's cards, links, buttons and marker buttons, and landmarks: header, main, "Parks" sidebar, "Map of parks" region.

In progress (Story 1.5, on a separate branch, not in this branch):

- Desktop sidebar layout (about 400px, independently scrolling, map filling the rest) — in progress (Story 1.5).
- Phone List / Map toggle — in progress (Story 1.5).
- Responsive and accessibility polish (375px with no horizontal scroll, touch targets, contrast pass) — in progress (Story 1.5).
- Full visual design from `design/screens/` applied — in progress (Story 1.5).

Until 1.5 lands, the list/details panel and the map are stacked one above the other at every width.

## Left out

- Search, filters and "near me" / current location (non-goals unless the core was done with time to spare; current location is deliberately never requested).
- Backend, accounts, hosted deployment, editing park data (non-goals).
- More than one photo per park: details show the first photo only, labelled "Photo 1 of N".
- Panning the map to the selected park.

## Decisions

- **Stack:** React + Vite, Tailwind CSS (mobile-first, design tokens in `src/index.css`), `react-leaflet` + Leaflet, Vitest + React Testing Library. No component library. Vitest 4 / jsdom 29 rather than the latest majors, because those declare engines that exclude Node 25.
- **Data load via `fetch`:** the planning spec said "static import", but a static import cannot fail, so it cannot show loading, error and retry states. The JSON is served from `public/data/parks.sample.json` and loaded with `fetch` (`src/data/parks.js`), then normalized once: null-safe, sorted A–Z, numbered 1–N. That number is shared by list cards and map markers.
- **Routing: React Router**, `/` is the list and `/parks/:id` is details. Details are linkable and browser Back works. This supersedes the `?park=` option in `design/README.md`.
- **Map: OpenStreetMap standard tiles** from `tile.openstreetmap.org` (keyless, attribution visible), and **`L.divIcon` markers** that contain a real `<button>`. A button gives an accessible name and Enter/Space for free, and divIcons avoid Leaflet's default marker images, which break under Vite without extra asset imports.
- **Phone vs desktop marker behaviour** is decided by viewport (`matchMedia('(min-width: 768px)')`): desktop opens details, phone opens a preview first so the map stays in view.
- **Focus return** uses the last-opened park id kept in App state (not router state), so it also works with browser Back.
- Where the design and the spec disagreed, the spec won (for example, missing fact tiles always use the muted dashed style).

## Assumptions

From `_bmad-output/specs/spec-find-local-parks/SPEC.md`:

- The municipality is the NYC-area set implied by the sample coordinates; the app is titled generically ("Find a Park").
- Parks without valid lat/lng still appear in the list and details, just without a marker.
- A data-load failure shows an error message, not a blank screen.

## Data changes

- **`parks.sample.json` is unchanged.** It is copied byte-for-byte from `requirements/parks.sample.json` to `public/data/parks.sample.json`. No fields were added and the schema was not extended.
- **Image URL transformation (at load time, in memory):** the sample image URLs point at `images.example.com`, which does not resolve. Each URL is mapped by filename stem to bundled pixel art in `src/assets/park-images/` (for example `https://images.example.com/prospect-1.jpg` → `src/assets/park-images/prospect-1.png`). A URL with no matching asset passes through unchanged, so the image-failure fallback still applies. The source data is not mutated. See `resolveImageUrl` in `src/data/parks.js`.

## Known issues

- **Cedar Hill Nature Preserve's coordinates look like a placeholder.** `40.7128, -74.006` is the commonly used "New York City" default point (near City Hall), not a nature preserve. It is plotted as given; not corrected.
- **OpenStreetMap tile usage policy:** the app loads tiles straight from `tile.openstreetmap.org`. That is fine for review and light use, but the OSM tile policy does not allow heavy or public production traffic. A public launch needs a tile provider or self-hosted tiles.
- Until Story 1.5 merges, the layout is a stacked baseline at every width (see "What works").
- `npm test` prints jsdom "Not implemented: Window's scrollTo() method" notices from React Router's scroll handling. They are console noise, not errors or failures.

## How it was checked

**Automated tests** (`npm test`: all passing, no unhandled errors). Each Story 1.6 acceptance criterion maps to a named test:

| What is covered | Test (file › name) |
|---|---|
| Old Mill: null description | `src/App.test.jsx` › Park details › "shows the description fallback for a null description" |
| Cedar Hill: empty images | `src/App.test.jsx` › Park details › "shows the no-photo box and no &lt;img&gt; for cedar-hill-nature-preserve" |
| Cedar Hill: null rating | `src/App.test.jsx` › Park details › "shows "Not yet rated" in the muted tile for a null rating, never 0" |
| East Ridge: empty images | `src/App.test.jsx` › Park details › "shows the no-photo box and no &lt;img&gt; for east-ridge-trailhead" |
| Highland: missing address | `src/App.test.jsx` › Park details › "shows "Address not listed" in muted text for a missing address" (also in Park list, and `src/data/parks.test.js` › "sets a missing nested address to null without throwing (Highland)") |
| An image that fails to load | `src/App.test.jsx` › Park details › "replaces a failing image with the no-photo box" |
| An unmatched image URL | `src/App.test.jsx` › Park details › "passes an unmatched image URL through and falls back when it fails"; `src/data/parks.test.js` › resolveImageUrl › "passes an unmatched URL through unchanged" |
| Keyboard flow: Tab to a card, Enter, heading focused, "All parks" returns focus to the card | `src/App.test.jsx` › Park details › "keyboard only: Tab to a card, Enter, heading focused, "All parks" returns focus to the card" |
| Image URL transformation | `src/data/parks.test.js` › resolveImageUrl › "maps a sample URL to the bundled png with the same stem" and "maps every sample image URL" |
| …without mutating the source data | `src/data/parks.test.js` › normalizeParks › "does not mutate the parsed source" (deep-frozen input) |
| Every sample park renders without "null"/"undefined"/"NaN" | `src/App.test.jsx` › Park details › "renders every sample park without null/undefined/NaN and with one h1" |
| Park → park without passing through the list | `src/App.test.jsx` › Map on desktop › "moves from a park with a failed image straight to another park via its marker" |
| No geolocation | `src/App.test.jsx` › Map of parks › "never calls navigator.geolocation"; `src/components/ParkMap.test.jsx` › "never requests the user location" |

Other tests cover the loading → error → retry flow, "Park not found", browser Back focus return, marker naming, numbering and highlighting, phone preview open/close/Escape, and the formatting helpers (`src/data/format.test.js`).

**Build:** `npm run build` succeeds.

**Manual checks** (listed in each story spec's Verification section, run with `npm run dev`): landmarks and "Park not found"; Tab through the list with the amber focus ring visible, Enter to open, Back/"All parks" to return to the same card; tiles, attribution and 12 markers; desktop marker click opens details with an amber marker; at 375px a marker tap shows the preview; no location prompt.

**Code review:** each story went through a BMAD code review (independent blind, edge-case and verification-gap reviewers). Findings and their verdicts are logged in the story specs under `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/` ("Review Triage Log"). Gaps a reviewer proved by mutation (removing code and seeing every test still pass) were patched with tests; the park → park gap deferred from Story 1.3 is closed by this story.

## Time spent

About 2 hours. Prompting started around 9 pm; the agents ran while I stepped away, with about 15 minutes of hands-on work before the first commit just before 10 pm, and I was still working at 11:25 pm.

## Next steps before public use

- Merge Story 1.5 (responsive layout, List/Map toggle, accessibility polish) and check at 375px and desktop on real devices.
- Move off the public OSM tile servers to a tile provider or self-hosted tiles that permit production traffic.
- Verify or correct the source data (Cedar Hill's coordinates, missing fields) with the parks department, and replace the placeholder image URLs with real photos and real alt text.
- Run a screen-reader pass (VoiceOver, NVDA) and an automated accessibility audit (axe) on the built app.
- Set a per-view `document.title`, and add an error boundary.
- Add browser end-to-end tests (Playwright) for the real layout, map interaction and focus behaviour, which jsdom cannot measure.
- Decide on hosting, caching and a data update process, since the JSON is a static file today.

## AI use & transcripts

This app was built with AI help, and the conversation logs are included unredacted:

- **Claude Code** (spec, stories, implementation, code reviews): `transcripts/`, exported by the developer before each fresh story context. Files start with a two-digit prefix giving the order they happened, then `storyN-dev`, `storyN-review`, or `storyN-dev-review` when one session did both; sessions not tied to a story use a short topic (`01-project-scaffolding`). Transcripts `08` onward follow the same convention.
  - `transcripts/01-project-scaffolding.txt`
  - `transcripts/02-story1-dev.txt`
  - `transcripts/03-story2-dev.txt`
  - `transcripts/04-story2-review.txt`
  - `transcripts/05-story3-dev.txt`
  - `transcripts/06-story3-review.txt`
  - `transcripts/07-story4-dev-review.txt`
- **Claude Design** (the UI designs): `transcripts/design-transcript.md`.
- **Transcript tooling side-quest** (writing the export script): `transcripts/tool-transcript.md`. The script itself is `tools/merge_session_transcripts.py`, which merges a Claude Code session's main and subagent transcripts into one timeline.

Planning and review artifacts produced along the way (spec, epics, story specs with review triage logs) are in `_bmad-output/`.

The pixel-art park images from the design session are bundled in `src/assets/park-images/`.

// jsdom has no matchMedia: stub it per test. `set` simulates a viewport change.
function stubViewport(desktop) {
  const listeners = new Set()
  const mql = {
    matches: desktop,
    media: '(min-width: 768px)',
    addEventListener: (_type, fn) => listeners.add(fn),
    removeEventListener: (_type, fn) => listeners.delete(fn),
        <BackButton />
        <App />
      </MemoryRouter>,
    )
    await user.click(await findMarkerByName('Prospect Park'))
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Prospect Park' })
    // Two clicks within 200 ms make Leaflet synthesize a map dblclick, which
    // jsdom's zero-size layout turns into a NaN LatLng. Move the clock past it.
    const later = Date.now() + 1000
    const clock = vi.spyOn(Date, 'now').mockReturnValue(later)
    try {
      await user.click(markerNamed('Prospect Park'))
    } finally {
      clock.mockRestore()
    }
    expect(screen.getByTestId('location')).toHaveTextContent('/parks/prospect-park')

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'back' }))
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'All parks' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByTestId('location')).toHaveTextContent(/^\/$/)
  })
